The shared matrix captures common attention patterns, while the residual component captures input-dependent variations. In this way, SGA avoids the query and key projections used in standard attention score computation, leading to linear time and score-matrix memory complexity with respect to the look-back length. We integrate SGA into several forecasting backbones and compare it with standard self-attention and lightweight attention variants on nine publicly available real-world datasets covering electricity, finance, weather, medical monitoring, human activity, and climate records. The results show that SGA improves inference efficiency on public benchmarks while maintaining competitive forecasting performance against state-of-the-art attention mechanisms. These benchmark results provide deployment-oriented evidence.

</details>


### [156] [DecompRL: Solving Harder Problems by Learning Modular Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02390)
*Juliette Decugis,Fabian Gloeckle,Francis Bach,Taco Cohen,Gabriel Synnaeve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DecompRL算法，通过强化学习让LLM学会将复杂问题分解为可独立实现的子模块，并通过重组子模块产生大量候选解（k^n），将计算瓶颈从GPU推理转移到CPU评估，在LiveCodeBench和CodeContests上以50倍更少的GPU token成本超越了标准RL基线，解决了标准生成方法无法触及的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（重复采样和RL）在面对基策略正确解概率接近零的问题时均会失败，因为搜索空间过大导致采样或梯度信号失效。作者希望通过分解问题为更小的可独立求解子函数来降低任务难度，从而突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出DecompRL，一种专门学习分解和实现层次化代码结构的RL算法。模型首先将问题分解为多个独立子模块，为每个模块生成k种实现，然后通过重组这些实现产生最多k^n个候选解，计算瓶颈从GPU推理转移至廉价的CPU评估。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench和CodeContests上（Qwen 2.5 7B和Code World Model 32B），DecompRL在每问题超过10^5 token时优于标准和多样性优化的RL基线，GPU token成本降低约50倍，能够解决标准生成方法无法解决的问题。

Conclusion: 通过分解-重组策略，模块化生成可指数级扩增搜索空间（k^n），将计算资源从昂贵的GPU推理转向廉价的CPU评估，是在超大规模搜索空间中突破LLM能力边界的有效途径。

Abstract: How can Large Language Models (LLMs) solve problems they currently cannot? Repeated sampling scales test-time compute but GPU cost grows linearly with attempts, while reinforcement learning (RL) with verifiable rewards improves single-attempt accuracy at the expense of sample diversity. Both strategies ultimately fail when the base policy has near-zero probability of producing a correct solution: no amount of sampling or gradient signal can overcome a search space that is simply too large. We take a different approach: rather than sampling harder, we make the task easier by decomposing problems into smaller, independently solvable sub-functions whose implementations can be recombined. Since off-the-shelf models are not trained for this modular generation, we introduce DecompRL, an RL algorithm that explicitly learns to decompose and implement hierarchical code structures. Recombining $k$ implementations of $n$ modules yields up to $k^{n}$ candidate solutions, shifting the bottleneck from GPU inference to cheap CPU evaluation and cutting GPU token cost by $\sim$50$\times$. On LiveCodeBench and CodeContests (Qwen~2.5~7B, Code World Model~32B), DecompRL outperforms standard and diversity-optimized RL baselines beyond $10^5$ tokens per problem, solving problems that standard generation cannot reach.

</details>


### [157] [Neuron-Aware Active Few-Shot Learning for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02423)
*Zhuowei Chen,Liwei Chen,Christian Schunn,Raquel Coelho,Xiang Lorraine Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NeuFS框架，利用神经元激活模式而非输出层信号进行主动少样本学习，通过双重标准选择策略（多样性+信息性）提升LLM在专业领域的适配效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动少样本学习方法依赖输出级信号（如预测熵、外部嵌入语义相似度）来选择样本，忽略了模型内部动态，无法精准定位模型的知识缺口。

Method: 提出NeuFS框架：（1）利用神经元激活模式直接表示样本；（2）设计双重选择策略——基于神经元模式的多样性选择确保样本覆盖，通过量化神经元共识识别LLM易产生幻觉的信息性/挑战性样本。

Result: 在三个数据集上的实验表明，NeuFS在推理和文本分类任务上均优于现有AFSL基线。消融实验证明内部神经元激活比外部嵌入提供更有效的选择信号。

Conclusion: NeuFS通过利用模型内部神经元动力学代替传统输出级代理信号，实现了更优的主动少样本学习效果，验证了内部神经元激活作为选择信号的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: Active Few-Shot Learning (AFSL) adapts LLMs to specialized domains by identifying the most valuable unlabeled samples for annotation and use as few-shot demonstrations, effectively reducing human annotation costs while promoting high performance. However, existing methods typically rely on output-level signals for sample identification, such as predictive entropy or semantic similarities with test-time data based on external embeddings, which often overlook models' internal dynamics, which could pinpoint specific knowledge gaps. To bridge this gap, we propose NeuFS, a Neuron-Aware Active Few-Shot Learning framework that shifts the selection paradigm from output-level proxies to models' internal dynamics. NeuFS utilizes neuron activation patterns to represent sample directly, and includes a dual-criteria selection strategy that: (1) ensures few-shot sample diversity with neuron patterns for broader example coverage, while (2) prioritizing on identifying informative and challenging few-shot samples LLMs tend to hallucinate by quantifying neuron consensus. Experiments on three datasets demonstrate that NeuFS excels in both reasoning and text classification tasks, outperforming existing AFSL baselines. Ablation studies further highlight that internal neuron activations provide a more principled and effective selection signal than external embeddings, validating the superiority of the proposed NeuFS.

</details>


### [158] [QFedAgent: Quantum-Enhanced Personalized Federated Learning for Multi-Agent Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02426)
*Quoc Bao Phan,Tuy Tan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QFedAgent是一个混合量子-经典个性化联邦学习框架，通过变分量子电路融合模块（仅需72个量子旋转参数，相比经典MLP融合的33K参数减少约10倍）处理多智能体异构非独立同分布多模态传感器数据，在OPPORTUNITY数据集上实现97.7%的平均测试精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在隐私敏感的机器人感知应用中具有潜力，但多智能体系统产生的异构非独立同分布多模态传感器数据会降低传统联邦学习算法性能，且经典融合模块带来大量参数开销和通信成本。

Method: 提出QFedAgent混合量子-经典个性化联邦学习框架，利用变分量子电路融合模块通过量子态编码和纠缠建模加速度计-陀螺仪交互，仅需72个量子旋转参数，相比经典MLP融合的33K参数实现约10倍参数压缩。

Result: 在OPPORTUNITY数据集上，针对基于主体的非独立同分布划分，达到97.7%的平均测试精度，验证了参数高效的量子融合与传统联邦基线相比具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 参数高效的量子融合模块在保持与传统联邦基线相当性能的同时，大幅降低了参数开销和通信成本，为多智能体活动识别中的隐私保护联邦学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed devices without sharing raw data, making it suitable for privacy-sensitive robotic sensing applications. However, multi-agent systems generate heterogeneous and non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) multimodal sensor streams that degrade conventional FL algorithms, while classical fusion modules introduce substantial parameter overhead and communication cost. This paper proposes QFedAgent, a hybrid quantum-classical personalized FL framework for multi-agent activity recognition. The approach integrates a variational quantum circuit fusion module that models accelerometer--gyroscope interactions through quantum state encoding and entanglement, requiring only 72 quantum rotation parameters versus 33K in classical multi-layer perceptron-based fusion, achieving approximately 10x total parameter reduction. Experiments on the OPPORTUNITY dataset under subject-based non-IID partitions demonstrate 97.7% mean test accuracy, confirming that parameter-efficient quantum fusion remains competitive with conventional federated baselines.

</details>


### [159] [Extreme Adaptive Transformer for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02437)
*Sanjeev Shrestha,Hui Liu,Yifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Exformer，一种通过极端自适应注意力机制（包含Local、Stride、Extreme三个稀疏组件）显式建模正常事件与极端事件间时序依赖的Transformer框架，在四个水文径流数据集上实现了优于基线模型的3天预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列中罕见但关键的极端事件常被现有Transformer模型忽略（因均匀处理所有时间点），而水文预测中极端洪峰对洪水监测、水资源管理和预警系统至关重要，亟需显式建模极端事件的预测方法。

Method: 提出Exformer框架，核心是极端自适应注意力机制，包含三个稀疏注意力组件：Local（捕获短期依赖）、Stride（捕获周期依赖）、Extreme（选择性建模正常与极端径流模式间的事件感知依赖）。

Result: 在四个真实水文径流数据集上，Exformer在3天预测任务中优于所有基线模型，证明了极端感知注意力对不平衡时间序列预测的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式引入极端感知注意力能够提升Transformer在不平衡时间序列（含罕见但重要事件）上的预测能力，为水文等极端事件敏感领域的时序预测提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Time series forecasting remains challenging when the underlying data contain rare but critical extreme events. This issue is particularly important in hydrologic forecasting, where streamflow distributions are often highly skewed and extreme peaks can have substantial impacts on flood monitoring, water resource management, and early warning systems. Although Transformer-based forecasting models have achieved strong performance by modeling long-range temporal dependencies, they typically treat all time points uniformly and may therefore underrepresent rare extreme patterns. In this paper, we propose the Extreme-Adaptive Transformer (Exformer), a forecasting framework designed to explicitly model temporal dependencies involving both normal and extreme events. Exformer introduces an extreme-adaptive attention mechanism composed of three sparse components: Local, Stride, and Extreme. The Local and Stride components capture short-term and periodic temporal dependencies, respectively, while the Extreme component selectively models event-aware dependencies between normal and extreme streamflow patterns. Experiments on four real-world hydrologic streamflow datasets show that Exformer achieves superior 3-day forecasting performance compared with state-of-the-art baselines. Our findings demonstrate that explicitly incorporating extreme-aware attention improves the forecasting capacity of Transformer models on imbalanced time series with rare but consequential events.

</details>


### [160] [Understanding the Robustness of Distributed Self-Supervised Learning Frameworks Against Non-IID Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02447)
*Xuanyu Chen,Nan Yang,Shuai Wang,Dong Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对分布式自监督学习（D-SSL）在非独立同分布（非IID）数据下的鲁棒性进行了严格的理论分析，证明掩码图像建模（MIM）比对比学习（CL）对异构数据更鲁棒，且分布式学习鲁棒性随网络平均连通性增加而提升，联邦学习（FL）不比分散式学习（DecL）差。基于理论提出MAR损失函数，实验验证了理论发现和MAR损失的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式自监督学习（D-SSL）面临数据异构性（非IID）这一关键挑战，但不同D-SSL框架对此挑战的理论响应机制尚不明确，缺乏严格的理论理解。

Method: 提出严谨的理论分析框架，对比掩码图像建模（MIM）与对比学习（CL）在非IID设置下的鲁棒性；分析网络拓扑连通性对鲁棒性的影响；基于理论推导提出MAR损失（MIM目标结合局部到全局对齐正则化）。

Result: 理论表明：1）MIM预训练相比CL对异构数据具有更强的内在鲁棒性；2）D-SSL鲁棒性随平均网络连通性增加而提升，联邦学习（FL）鲁棒性不弱于分散式学习（DecL）；3）MAR损失在多种模型架构和分布式设置下有效。

Conclusion: 为未来D-SSL算法设计提供了坚实的理论基础，并通过MAR损失验证了理论的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Recent research has introduced distributed self-supervised learning (D-SSL) approaches to leverage vast amounts of unlabeled decentralized data. However, D-SSL faces the critical challenge of data heterogeneity, and there is limited theoretical understanding of how different D-SSL frameworks respond to this challenge. To fill this gap, we present a rigorous theoretical analysis of the robustness of D-SSL frameworks under non-IID (non-independent and identically distributed) settings. Our results show that pre-training with Masked Image Modeling (MIM) is inherently more robust to heterogeneous data than Contrastive Learning (CL), and that the robustness of decentralized SSL increases with average network connectivity, implying that federated learning (FL) is no less robust than decentralized learning (DecL). These findings provide a solid theoretical foundation for guiding the design of future D-SSL algorithms. To further illustrate the practical implications of our theory, we introduce MAR loss, a refinement of the MIM objective with local-to-global alignment regularization. Extensive experiments across model architectures and distributed settings validate our theoretical insights, and additionally confirm the effectiveness of MAR loss as an application of our analysis.

</details>


### [161] [Neuron-Aware Data Selection for Annotation-Free LLM Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02460)
*Zhuowei Chen,Xiang Lorraine Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Neuron-OPSD，一种基于神经元激活的无注释自蒸馏框架，通过利用内部神经元激活指导训练数据选择和教师上下文构建，然后通过教师分布的在线策略蒸馏训练模型，在提升领域内任务性能的同时保持跨域泛化能力并缓解校准误差膨胀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在缺乏真实交互反馈或人类标注监督的情况下微调大语言模型仍然具有挑战性，特别是在专家标注成本高昂的专业领域。现有的无注释自进化方法存在领域外性能下降或校准误差膨胀等问题。

Method: 提出Neuron-OPSD框架，利用内部神经元激活指导训练数据选择和教师上下文构建，通过在线策略蒸馏从教师分布中训练模型，无需任何阶段的地面真实标签。

Result: 在专业领域基准测试中，Neuron-OPSD提升了领域内任务性能，同时保持了跨域泛化能力，并缓解了校准误差膨胀问题。

Conclusion: Neuron-OPSD适用于在线交互或外部监督成本高昂或不可行的场景，在概念上区别于依赖已标注奖励轨迹的离线RL方法。

Abstract: Post-training large language models (LLMs) without real-world interaction feedback or human-labeled supervision remains challenging, particularly in specialized domains where expert annotations are costly to obtain. Recent annotation-free self-evolution methods address this by using the model's own outputs as supervision signals, constructing a teacher via additional context and aggregating predictions across multiple rollouts through majority voting to produce pseudo-labels. However, these approaches are not without drawbacks: SFT- and GRPO-based variants suffer out-of-domain performance degradation, while reward-based on-policy RL inflates calibration error. In this paper, we propose Neuron On-Policy Self-Distillation (Neuron-OPSD), a data-centric framework for annotation-free self-distillation that leverages internal neuron activations to guide both training-data selection and teacher context construction. The model is then trained via on-policy distillation from the teacher distribution, requiring no ground-truth labels at any stage. Across specialized-domain benchmarks, Neuron-OPSD improves in-domain task performance while preserving cross-domain generalization and mitigating calibration collapse over prior annotation-free baselines. This framework is particularly relevant to settings where online interaction or external supervision is costly or infeasible, and is conceptually distinct from offline RL approaches that rely on logged, reward-labeled trajectories.

</details>


### [162] [DemoPSD: Disagreement-Modulated Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02502)
*Yunhe Li,Hao Shi,Wenhao Liu,Mengzhe Ruan,Hanxu Hou,Zhongxiang Dai,Shuang Qiu,Linqi Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DemoPSD通过选择性采用教师指导，构建逆向KL重心目标（教师与学生分布的加权几何组合），解决了on-policy自蒸馏中特权信息泄露和探索能力被抑制的问题，在科学推理基准上优于GRPO和SDPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有on-policy自蒸馏方法中，教师的密集token级监督基于特权信息（如完整推理路径），导致学生编码了测试时不可用的答案相关捷径（特权信息泄露），且过度拟合域内模式、抑制探索、损害跨域泛化。

Method: 提出DemoPSD框架，核心思想是选择性采用教师指导：不拟合完整教师分布，而是将学生导向逆向KL重心目标——教师分布与学生分布的加权几何组合。通过测量两分布间的差异，自适应控制每个token位置上的混合权重。理论证明该方法可实现泄露衰减和探索保持。

Result: 在SciKnowEval四个科学领域上，DemoPSD优于GRPO和SDPO，且保持更高的训练熵，在OOD的GPQA基准上展现出鲁棒的泛化能力。

Conclusion: DemoPSD有效缓解了特权信息泄露问题，在保持探索能力的同时提升了泛化性能，为LLM推理训练中的自蒸馏范式提供了更稳健的方案。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) has emerged as a practical method for training large language models (LLMs) to reason, where a single model acts as both the teacher and the student with different levels of information access. However, recent studies have found that the teacher's dense token-level supervision, conditioned on privileged information, can lead to overfitting to in-domain patterns, suppress exploration, and hurt cross-domain generalization, while also introducing a more fundamental issue: *privileged information leakage*, where the student encodes answer-dependent shortcuts that are unavailable at test time. We introduce **DemoPSD**, a novel framework that resolves such problems through the idea of *selective adoption of teacher guidance*. Instead of fitting the full teacher distribution, DemoPSD steers the student toward a *reverse-KL barycenter target*, a weighted geometric combination of the teacher and student distributions, that naturally balances learning from the teacher with preserving the student's own reasoning capacity. We measure the difference between their distributions and use such a discrepancy to adaptively control the blending at each token position. We provably show that DemoPSD achieves **(1)** *leakage attenuation*, i.e., effective mitigation of privileged information leakage; and **(2)** *exploration preservation*, i.e., preservation of exploration capacity under dense token-level distillation. Extensive experiments on SciKnowEval across four scientific fields show that DemoPSD outperforms both GRPO and SDPO while maintaining higher training entropy and robustly generalizing to out-of-distribution GPQA benchmarks.

</details>


### [163] [Program-as-Weights: A Programming Paradigm for Fuzzy Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02512)
*Wentao Zhang,Liliana Hotsko,Woojeong Kim,Pengyu Nie,Stuart Shieber,Yuntian Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"模糊函数编程"范式，通过PAW方法将自然语言规范编译为轻量级神经工件，在MacBook M3上以30 tokens/s运行，匹配32B模型性能但仅需1/50推理内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常编程任务（如日志告警、JSON修复、搜索结果排序）难以用规则实现，目前依赖大语言模型API，但存在延迟、不可复现和高成本问题，亟需一种本地、可复现且经济的替代方案。

Method: 提出Program-as-Weights (PAW)：一个4B编译器在10M样本的FuzzyBench上训练，为冻结的轻量级解释器生成参数高效适配器（LoRA等），实现从自然语言到紧凑神经工件的编译。

Result: 使用0.6B Qwen3解释器执行PAW程序，匹配直接提示Qwen3-32B的性能，推理内存仅需约1/50，在MacBook M3上达到30 tokens/s。

Conclusion: PAW将基础模型从每次输入的问题求解器转变为工具构建器：一次编译生成可重用的轻量级工件，后续每次函数调用都廉价且可离线执行。

Abstract: Many everyday programming tasks resist clean rule-based implementation, such as alerting on important log lines, repairing malformed JSON, or ranking search results by intent, and are increasingly outsourced to large language model APIs at the cost of locality, reproducibility, and price. We propose fuzzy-function programming: compiling such a function from a natural-language specification into a compact, locally-executable neural artifact. We instantiate this paradigm with Program-as-Weights (PAW), in which a 4B compiler trained on FuzzyBench, a 10M-example dataset we release, emits parameter-efficient adapters for a frozen, lightweight interpreter. A 0.6B Qwen3 interpreter executing PAW programs matches the performance of direct prompting of Qwen3-32B, while using roughly one fiftieth of the inference memory and running at 30 tokens/s on a MacBook M3. PAW reframes the foundation model from a per-input problem solver into a tool builder: invoked once per function definition, it produces a small reusable artifact whose subsequent calls per function application are cheap and offline.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [Inflation from Covariant Signature Change: A Geometric Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01274)
*Raghvendra Singh,Sergey Bondarenko*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种协变机制，通过度规符号从欧几里得到洛伦兹区域的平滑变化，驱动有限时段的加速膨胀。该转变由沿类时曲线的标量插值器编码，发生在余维一超曲面上，该处度规退化但曲率不变量有限，因此曲率正则。通过协变延拓，将爱因斯坦张量重写为局域化有效源，在洛伦兹区域导出与模型无关的加速判据：当插值器斜率超过由外曲率和空间Ricci曲率确定的临界值时膨胀持续，首次达到该值时结束。标准平滑轮廓可给出加速时段闭式表达式，表明轮廓形状控制持续时间。该构造提供了从正则欧几里得原点到早期洛伦兹相的无奇点、无暴胀子路径，与无边界边界条件兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决宇宙早期起源中如何从欧几里得（虚时间）平滑过渡到洛伦兹（实时间）并自然产生有限时段加速膨胀（暴胀）的几何动力学问题，避免引入暴胀子标量场及初始奇点。

Method: 采用协变度规符号转变机制，通过沿类时曲线的标量插值器编码度规从欧几里得到洛伦兹的连续变化；将转变后的爱因斯坦张量重写为依赖于插值器的局域化有效源；在洛伦兹区域推导加速判据；通过tanh、广义逻辑函数、幂律/反正切等标准平滑轮廓求解放射时段闭式表达式。

Result: 导出了模型无关的局部加速判据：加速膨胀持续当插值器斜率超过由外曲率和初始超曲面空间Ricci曲率决定的临界值，并在该不等式首次饱和时结束。标准平滑轮廓给出加速时段解析表达式，表明轮廓形状控制持续时间。

Conclusion: 该构造提供了从正则欧几里得起点到洛伦兹加速膨胀相的无奇点、无暴胀子路径，与无边界边界条件相容，为宇宙早期起源提供了新的几何动力学框架。

Abstract: We present a covariant mechanism in which a smooth change of metric signature, from a Euclidean to a Lorentzian regime, drives a finite interval of accelerated expansion. The transition, encoded by a scalar interpolator along a timelike congruence, occurs on a codimension-one hypersurface where the continued metric is degenerate but curvature invariants remain finite, so the surface is curvature-regular. Using this covariant continuation, we rewrite the Einstein tensor of the continued metric as a localized, interpolator-dependent effective source for the post-transition Lorentzian branch, yielding a purely geometric stress tensor supported near the crossing. In the Lorentzian regime, we derive a model-independent, local criterion for acceleration: inflation persists while the interpolator's slope exceeds a critical value fixed by the extrinsic curvature and the spatial Ricci curvature on the initial hypersurface, and ends when this inequality is first saturated. Standard smooth profiles (tanh, generalized logistic, and power-law/arctan) admit closed-form expressions for the proper-time duration of the accelerated epoch, showing that, for fixed geometric data, the profile shape controls this duration. The construction provides a non-singular, inflaton-free route from a regular Euclidean origin to an early Lorentzian phase of accelerated expansion, in a manner compatible with no--boundary--type boundary conditions.

</details>


### [165] [Hypersurface Anchored Variational Principle for General Relativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01285)
*Prasanta Sahoo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种超曲面锚定的变分推广广义相对论，通过在爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量中添加独立于时空度规变分的嵌入类空超曲面泛函，导出了包含局域化分布贡献的爱因斯坦方程和确定可允许嵌入的锚定方程。该构造在保持标准二阶主结构和不引入额外传播自由度的前提下，定义了广义相对论经典解空间的一个约束扇区，其锚定条件不等价于局部切片规范条件，是一种真正的变分限制而非坐标选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构建一种超曲面锚定的变分框架，使得嵌入超曲面的变分独立于时空度规，从而在广义相对论中引入一种新的变分约束，该约束不同于传统的规范固定条件，能够定义广义相对论解空间的一个子扇区。

Method: 在爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量中补充一个微分同胚不变的超曲面泛函，超曲面的嵌入变分独立于时空度规变分；通过变分原理导出欧拉-拉格朗日方程组，包括带有局域化分布贡献的爱因斯坦方程和锚定方程；利用椭圆性和可逆性假设，通过隐函数定理和椭圆估计建立锚定超曲面的局部持久性和线性稳定性。

Result: 体场方程在超曲面外保持标准二阶主结构，不引入额外传播自由度；锚定条件不等价于局部切片规范条件；在正则形式下，动量约束保持标准形式，哈密顿约束获得超曲面支撑项；对易子代数在体区域恢复狄拉克代数，偏差仅限于局域化分布面贡献；在均匀宇宙学中，局域化项在锚定面上诱导匹配条件，允许膨胀率的有限跃迁。

Conclusion: 该构造定义了广义相对论经典解空间的一个约束扇区，由至少包含一个满足锚定方程的嵌入超曲面的时空组成，是一种真正的变分限制而非坐标选择。

Abstract: A hypersurface anchored variational extension of General Relativity is formulated in which the Einstein-Hilbert action is supplemented by a diffeomorphism invariant functional supported on an embedded spacelike hypersurface whose embedding is varied independently of the spacetime metric. The resulting Euler-Lagrange system consists of the Einstein equations with a localized distributional contribution together with an anchoring equation determining admissible embeddings. For the admissible class of hypersurface functionals considered here, the bulk field equations retain the standard second order principal structure away from the hypersurface and no additional propagating bulk gravitational degrees of freedom are introduced. Under ellipticity and invertibility assumptions, local persistence and linear stability of anchoring hypersurfaces follow from standard implicit function and elliptic estimates. The anchoring condition is generically inequivalent to local slicing gauge conditions and therefore defines a genuine variational restriction rather than a coordinate choice. In the canonical formulation, the momentum constraints retain their standard form, whereas the Hamiltonian constraint acquires a hypersurface supported term. The corresponding smeared generators close in the weak sense: the Dirac algebra is recovered in the bulk, with deviations confined to localized distributional surface contributions. The construction therefore defines a constrained sector of the classical solution space of General Relativity consisting of spacetimes that admit at least one embedded hypersurface satisfying the anchoring equation. In homogeneous cosmology, the localized term induces matching conditions across the anchoring surface, allowing finite transitions in the expansion rate while preserving standard evolution away from the transition hypersurface.

</details>


### [166] [Impact of Spacecraft Orbit Uncertainties and Velocity Mismodeling on the LISA Gravitational-Wave Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01318)
*Lorenzo Speri,Olaf Hartwig,Waldemar Martens,Oliver Jennrich,Eric Joffre,Michele Armano,Martin Hewitson,Nora Lützgendorf*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了LISA空间引力波探测器中航天器轨道不确定性对探测器响应的影响，以及忽略航天器速度带来的误差。结果表明轨道不确定性的影响在高频段最差失配度低于10^{-7}，忽略速度的影响在10^{-4} Hz附近最大（失配度约10^{-4}），对信噪比200的银河系双星系统观测一年导致参数估计偏差小于1-σ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA数据处理研究通常假设航天器轨道已知，但实际上轨道确定过程会引入位置和速度的不确定性，需要量化这些不确定性如何影响探测器输出和引力波数据分析。

Method: 通过理论分析量化轨道不确定性和忽略航天器速度对LISA响应的影响，使用失配度（mismatch）作为误差度量，并进一步评估这些误差对引力波参数估计的偏置影响。

Result: 轨道不确定性对高频段影响最大，最差失配度低于10^{-7}；忽略航天器速度在10^{-4} Hz附近影响最大，失配度约10^{-4}；对于频率10^{-4} Hz、SNR=200、观测一年的银河系双星，忽略速度导致的参数估计偏差小于1-σ。

Conclusion: 首次完整刻画了从引力波应变经探测器输出到参数估计整个链条中LISA响应误差的传播特征。

Abstract: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) is a space-based gravitational wave observatory that consists of three spacecraft in a near-equilateral triangular formation. The spacecraft orbits are typically assumed to be perfectly known in LISA data analysis studies, but in reality, the orbit determination process introduces uncertainties in the spacecraft positions and velocities. In this work, we investigate how these uncertainties propagate into the LISA detector output and the impact of neglecting the spacecraft velocities. We quantify these errors in the knowledge of the LISA response using mismatches and discuss the implications for gravitational wave data analysis. We find that spacecraft orbit uncertainties impact the LISA response knowledge at high frequencies with worst mismatch below $10^{-7}$. The effect of neglecting the spacecraft velocities is largest at frequencies around $10^{-4}$ Hz with mismatches of order $10^{-4}$. For a galactic binary with frequency $10^{-4}$ Hz and SNR=200 observed for one year, we find that neglecting the spacecraft velocities in the response leads to less than 1-$σ$ biases in the parameter estimates. This work provides the first characterization of how errors in the LISA gravitational wave response propagate from gravitational wave strain through detector output to estimated parameters.

</details>


### [167] [Vacuum Cherenkov radiation in supercritical magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01419)
*Daniel Gálvez-García,Nora Bretón*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在极强电磁场下真空折射率改变导致光速低于c的现象，超相对论带电粒子可超光速并产生切伦科夫辐射。比较了Euler-Heisenberg理论在临界与超临界磁场下的切伦科夫辐射，以及切伦科夫辐射与同步辐射的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在极强电磁场中真空极化效应会改变真空折射率，使光速变慢，超相对论带电粒子可能超光速并产生切伦科夫辐射。现有研究对临界与超临界磁场下切伦科夫辐射的特征差异以及其与同步辐射的对比尚未充分探讨。

Method: 基于Euler-Heisenberg有效场理论，分别在临界磁场和超临界磁场条件下，计算并比较切伦科夫辐射的性质；同时将切伦科夫辐射与同步辐射进行对比分析。

Result: 给出了临界与超临界磁场条件下切伦科夫辐射的对比结果，以及切伦科夫辐射与同步辐射的差异比较。

Conclusion: 在极强磁场下真空折射率改变会引发切伦科夫辐射，且该辐射特性在临界与超临界磁场条件下存在差异；切伦科夫辐射与同步辐射在强磁场环境中表现出不同的物理特征。

Abstract: In the presence of very intense electromagnetic fields, the refractive index of vacuum is modified such that light velocity is less than $c$ and ultrarelativistic charged particles can be faster than light and can induce Cherenkov radiation. We present the comparison of the Cherenkov radiation produced by the Euler-Heisenberg theory for critical and supercritical magnetic fields. We also make the comparison between the Cherenkov and the synchrotron radiation produced by the charged particles.

</details>


### [168] [Stability regions of glued wormholes with massless Kim-Lee backreacted spacetimes as interior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01687)
*G. F. Akhtaryanova,R. Kh. Karimov,R. N. Izmailov,A. Bhattacharya*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过薄壳将零ADM质量的内部虫洞（如Kim-Lee虫洞）与大质量外部时空（Schwarzschild和Reissner-Nordström）粘合后形成的"粘合虫洞"的稳定性，发现两种情况下稳定区域几乎相似，尽管内部时空物理性质不同（分别带有标量荷和电荷）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零ADM质量虫洞（如Ellis-Bronnikov虫洞）在天体物理应用（如星系微引力透镜）中具有重要意义，但单独存在时对小扰动不稳定。研究者希望通过将内部零质量虫洞与外部大质量时空粘合，利用薄壳动力学来获得稳定的可观测虫洞构型。

Method: 采用Garcia、Lobo和Visser提出的薄壳"质量"与"外力"约束概念，分析粘合虫洞的稳定性。考虑两类构型：内部为Kim-Lee虫洞，分别与外部Schwarzschild真空和Reissner-Nordström时空粘合，通过薄壳动力学划分稳定区域。

Result: 两种构型的稳定区域几乎相似，尽管两种Kim-Lee内部时空物理性质截然不同（一种带标量荷，另一种带电）。两种粘合虫洞的稳定性条件被详细分析并确定。

Conclusion: 通过薄壳粘合的方式，可以使原本不稳定的零ADM质量虫洞作为内部伙伴获得稳定性，且稳定性质对内部时空的具体物理特性（标量荷vs电荷）不敏感，显示出一定的普适性。

Abstract: Asymptotic zero Arnowitt-Deser-Misner (ADM) mass wormholes, such as the zero-mass traversable Ellis-Bronnikov wormhole, are of great interest for astrophysical applications such as in the galactic microlensing. However, when considered individually, they are unstable to small perturbations. On the other hand, there is a possibility that they can be stable as an interior partner of a traversable glued wormhole obtained by suitably gluing the interior to the observationally relevant massive exterior spacetimes across spherically symmetric thin shells. Although the exterior spacetime has non-zero ADM mass, massless interior partner remains massless sharing the stability of the glued wormhole. The dynamics of the thin-shell then demarcates the stability regions of the glued wormhole that we wish to study here by employing the novel concepts of thin-shell "mass" and of "external force" constraints discovered by Garcia, Lobo and Visser. We shall consider two classes, where the zero ADM mass interior are Kim-Lee wormholes glued to the exterior Schwarzschild vacuum and Reissner-Nordström spacetime respectively. It turns out that the stability regions in both cases are almost similar although the two interior Kim-Lee spacetimes are physically very different, one scalar charged and the other electrically charged. The conditions under which the stability of glued wormholes could be achieved are analyzed in detail.

</details>


### [169] [Particle dynamics and quasi-periodic oscillations of a Reissner--Nordström-like black hole in Kalb--Ramond gravity under an external magnetic test field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01723)
*Faizuddin Ahmed,Ahmad Al-Badawi,Sardor Murodov,Bekzod Rahmatov,Javlon Rayimbaev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Kalb-Ramond引力中带电Reissner-Nordström-like黑洞周围带电试验粒子的动力学和准周期振荡，通过洛伦兹破缺参数ℓ和外部磁场的影响，利用GRO J1655—40、XTE J1550—564和M82 X-1的观测数据进行MCMC分析，发现该模型能一致地再现观测QPO对，表明QPO可作为探测洛伦兹破缺黑洞几何和强引力电磁效应的有效工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Kalb-Ramond引力中洛伦兹破缺效应与外部磁场对带电黑洞周围粒子动力学及准周期振荡的联合影响，并利用实际观测数据约束模型参数，检验该理论框架在强引力环境中的适用性。

Method: 在带电KR黑洞背景下，从无源麦克斯韦方程构建与修正几何自洽的磁场构型；推导带电粒子的运动方程、有效势、圆轨道条件及轨道和径向epicyclic频率；采用相对论进动模型将上下QPO频率分别识别为轨道频率和近星点进动频率；利用GRO J1655—40、XTE J1550—564、M82 X-1的观测QPO数据，通过MCMC分析约束模型参数。

Result: 黑洞电荷Q/M、KR参数ℓ、粒子比电荷ε、磁耦合β=bM共同影响ISCO和QPO频率；MCMC后验约束表明带电KR黑洞模型能在所选参数范围内一致再现观测QPO对。

Conclusion: QPO观测可作为探测洛伦兹破缺黑洞几何和强引力电磁效应的有效现象学工具。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of charged test particles and quasi-periodic oscillations around a Reissner--Nordström-like black hole in Kalb--Ramond (KR) gravity in the presence of an external magnetic test field. The KR background introduces a Lorentz-violating parameter $\ell$, which modifies the spacetime geometry, horizon structure, circular orbits, and characteristic frequencies of particle motion. In contrast to the standard Wald-type prescription, the magnetic-field configuration is constructed from the source-free Maxwell equation on the charged KR background, allowing the magnetic profile to be consistently adapted to the modified geometry. We derive the equations of motion, the effective potential, the conditions for circular orbits, and the orbital and radial epicyclic frequencies of charged particles. The results show that the black-hole charge $Q/M$, the KR parameter $\ell$, the specific particle charge $ε$, and the magnetic coupling $β=bM$ jointly affect the innermost stable circular orbit (ISCO) and the quasi-periodic oscillation (QPO) frequencies. We then apply the obtained frequencies to the relativistic precession model, where the upper QPO frequency is identified with the orbital frequency and the lower one with the periastron-precession frequency. Using the observed twin-peak QPO data of GRO J1655--40, XTE J1550--564, and M82 X-1, we perform a Markov chain Monte Carlo analysis to constrain the model parameters. The obtained posterior constraints indicate that the charged KR black-hole model with an external magnetic field can consistently reproduce the observed QPO pairs within the adopted parameter ranges. These findings suggest that QPO observations may serve as a useful phenomenological tool for probing Lorentz-violating black-hole geometries and electromagnetic effects in strong-gravity environments.

</details>


### [170] [Classical and Loop Quantum Cosmology of Interacting Dark Energy: A Dynamical System Analysis with Superfluid Dark Matter and Dust Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01724)
*Mohd Shahalam,K. Yerzhanov,G. Bauyrzhan,P. K. Dhankar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在经典爱因斯坦引力与圈量子宇宙学框架下，研究了具有不同状态方程的相互作用暗能量与暗物质（超流暗物质和无压流体）的宇宙学动力学。经典引力中无压物质模型存在稳定吸引子，而超流暗物质仅有鞍点；圈量子宇宙学中量子反弹消除了经典吸引子，所有模型仅余鞍点与非双曲临界点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究暗物质属性（超流暗物质 vs. 无压流体）以及量子引力效应（圈量子宇宙学）如何影响相互作用暗能量-暗物质系统的渐近演化行为。

Method: 运用动力系统理论，在两种引力理论（经典Einstein引力与圈量子宇宙学）中，考虑两种暗物质模型（超流暗物质用广义立方状态方程描述、标准无压流体）和两种暗能量模型（广义非线性状态方程与常数状态方程），并引入两个唯象相互作用项 $Q=α\dotρ_m$ 与 $Q=β\dotρ_d$ 进行分析。

Result: 经典引力下，无压物质模型存在稳定的晚期吸引子，超流暗物质模型仅有鞍点与非双曲临界点。圈量子宇宙学中，量子几何修正消除了无压物质模型的稳定吸引子，所有相互作用模型仅存在鞍点与非双曲临界点。

Conclusion: 暗物质的状态方程性质以及量子引力效应均对相互作用暗区系统的渐近演化产生显著影响，圈量子宇宙学的量子反弹从根本上改变了相空间动力学结构。

Abstract: We study the cosmological dynamics of interacting dark energy and dark matter in Classical Einstein Gravity and Loop Quantum Cosmology. Two dark matter scenarios are considered: superfluid dark matter described by a generalized cubic equation of state and the standard pressureless fluid. The dark energy component is modeled using both a generalized nonlinear equation of state and a constant equation of state. We examine two phenomenological interaction terms, $Q=α\dotρ_m$ and $Q=β\dotρ_d$, which govern the energy transfer between the dark sectors. In classical gravity, the pressureless matter model exhibits stable late-time attractors, whereas the superfluid dark matter model admits only saddle and non-hyperbolic critical points. Extending the analysis to Loop Quantum Cosmology, quantum geometric corrections replace the Big Bang singularity with a nonsingular quantum bounce, and significantly modify the phase-space dynamics. As a result, the stable attractors of the classical pressureless matter model disappear, and all interacting models possess only saddle and non-hyperbolic critical points. These findings highlight the significant influence of both dark matter properties and quantum gravitational effects on the asymptotic evolution of interacting dark-sectors.

</details>


### [171] [Rotating Black Holes and the Kerr/CFT Correspondence in Einstein-Bumblebee Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01884)
*Yu-Qi Chen,Jin-Yang Shen,Hai-Shan Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在五维Einstein-Bumblebee引力中构造了等角动量旋转黑洞（含/不含宇宙常数），通过Wald形式与Komar积分两种方法研究热力学性质。两种方法计算的守恒量（质量、角动量、熵）相差一个仅由Bumblebee耦合常数决定的预因子。应用Kerr/CFT对偶导出的微观熵精确复现了Komar积分版本的熵，而非Wald熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在修正引力理论（Einstein-Bumblebee引力）中研究旋转黑洞的热力学性质，并探讨Wald形式与Komar积分两种方法在计算守恒量时的差异。同时，通过Kerr/CFT对偶检验微观熵的对应关系，以揭示该理论中黑洞热力学的正确描述框架。

Method: 1. 构造五维Einstein-Bumblebee引力中的等角动量旋转黑洞解（含/不含宇宙常数）。2. 分别利用Wald formalism和Komar积分计算黑洞的质量、角动量和熵。3. 应用Kerr/CFT对偶计算微观熵，并与前两种宏观熵结果对比。

Result: 1. Wald形式与Komar积分给出的守恒量相差一个仅由Bumblebee耦合常数决定的常数预因子。2. Kerr/CFT对偶导出的微观熵精确等于Komar积分版本的熵，而非Wald熵。

Conclusion: 在Einstein-Bumblebee引力中，Komar积分（而非Wald形式）给出的熵与Kerr/CFT对偶的微观熵一致，表明Komar积分可能是描述该理论中黑洞热力学的更合适方法。

Abstract: We constructed rotating black holes with equal angular momentum in five dimensional Einstein-Bumblebee gravity with and without cosmological constant. Their thermodynamic properties are examined via two distinct methods: the Wald formalism and the Komar integral. Notably, the conserved charges, including mass, angular momentum, and entropy, computed from these two approaches differ by a constant prefactor that is solely determined by the Bumblebee coupling. Subsequently, we apply the Kerr/CFT correspondence to derive the microscopic entropy of these black holes and find that it precisely reproduces the entropy in Komar-integral version, rather than the Wald entropy.

</details>


### [172] [Chaotic behaviors of particles around the black hole with an anisotropic matter immersed in a magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01910)
*Khusan Alibekov,Hocheol Lee,Yovqochev Pahlavon,Bobomurat Ahmedov,Bum-Hoon Lee,Ahmadjon Abdujabbarov,Wonwoo Lee*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过Harrison变换得到爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦方程的精确解，描述了一个静态黑洞与各向异性物质共存于外部磁场中的系统。研究发现：各向异性物质参数增大抑制局部混沌（Lyapunov指数减小），而外部磁场变化引发全局混沌行为的定性转变（通过庞加莱截面展示规则与混沌轨迹的转换）。这些因素在塑造黑洞周围混沌粒子动力学中起互补作用，为研究磁化黑洞时空中的不可积粒子运动及探测银河系中心黑洞提供了理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索各向异性物质和外部磁场对黑洞周围混沌粒子动力学的协同影响，为理解磁化黑洞时空中的非可积粒子运动提供理论框架，并服务于银河系中心黑洞的观测研究。

Method: 采用Harrison变换求解爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦方程，获得描述静态黑洞、各向异性物质和外部磁场共存的精确解；通过Lyapunov指数分析局部混沌，利用庞加莱截面分析全局混沌行为。

Result: 各向异性物质参数增加会抑制局部混沌（Lyapunov指数减小）；外部磁场变化引起全局混沌的定性转变（庞加莱截面显示规则与混沌轨迹的转换）；两种因素在塑造混沌动力学中发挥不同但互补的作用。

Conclusion: 该研究为磁化黑洞时空中的非可积粒子运动提供了新的理论框架，有助于探测银河系中心黑洞，其中磁场可能来自天体物理黑洞周围的等离子体效应。

Abstract: We present an exact solution to the Einstein-Maxwell equations that describes a static black hole coexisting with anisotropic matter immersed in an external magnetic field, obtained via the Harrison transformation. Our findings reveal that an increase in the anisotropic matter parameter systematically suppresses the local chaotic behavior, as indicated by a reduction in the Lyapunov exponent. Conversely, variations in the external magnetic field lead to qualitative changes in global chaotic behavior. This is analyzed through Poincaré sections, which demonstrate transitions between regular and chaotic trajectories resulting from the nonlinear gravitational-magnetic interactions. These factors play distinct yet complementary roles in shaping chaotic particle dynamics around black holes. This study would offer a new theoretical framework for exploring non-integrable particle motion within magnetized black hole spacetimes and for probing a black hole at the galactic center, where magnetic fields may arise from plasma effects surrounding astrophysical black holes.

</details>


### [173] [Phase Transitions with Lyapunov Exponents under Einstein and String Frames in Dilatonic Reissner--Nordström--AdS Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01912)
*Hocheol Lee,Bogeun Gwak*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了膨胀Reissner-Nordström-AdS黑洞中Lyapunov指数作为相变动力学探针的有效性，发现尽管Lyapunov指数本身在共形框架变换下具有非平凡依赖性，但其捕捉的相结构特征（包括尖点行为和相变点）在不同框架下保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Lyapunov指数能否作为黑洞相变的动力学探针，并考察其在共形框架变换下的行为，以区分框架依赖的数值与框架无关的临界结构特征。

Method: 在Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton理论框架下，分析膨胀Reissner-Nordström-AdS黑洞的热力学量（Hawking温度、Wald熵）和带电试探粒子的Lyapunov指数，分别在Einstein标架和String标架下进行数值计算与比较，同时考察有质量和无质量两种极限情况。

Result: 热力学量在共形框架变换下保持不变，因此两框架下相结构完全一致；Lyapunov指数对于有质量试探粒子表现出非平凡的框架依赖性（源于膨胀耦合），但在无质量极限下无框架依赖性；数值分析表明，Lyapunov指数捕捉的相结构特征（尖点行为、相变点）与框架选择无关。

Conclusion: Lyapunov指数本身具有框架依赖性，但其表征的临界相结构在不同共形框架下保持恒定，因此可作为黑洞相变的稳健动力学探针。

Abstract: We investigate Lyapunov exponents as dynamical probes of black hole phase transitions in dilatonic Reissner--Nordström--AdS black holes within Einstein--Maxwell--dilaton theory. The thermodynamic quantities and the Lyapunov exponent of charged probe particles were analyzed in both the Einstein and string frames, thus providing a direct comparison between the thermodynamic phase structure of the black hole and that captured by the Lyapunov exponent. Thermodynamic quantities, including the Hawking temperature and Wald entropy, remained constant under conformal frame transformations, yielding identical phase structures in the two frames. In contrast, the Lyapunov exponent exhibited non-trivial frame dependence for massive probe particles due to dilaton coupling, while no frame dependence was found in the massless limit. Numerical analysis revealed that the phase structure features captured by the Lyapunov exponent, including characteristic cusp behavior and transition points, were independent of the choice of frame, despite the Lyapunov exponent itself being frame-dependent. Therefore, the Lyapunov exponent exhibited frame-dependent values, while the critical structure it captures remained constant across conformal frames.

</details>


### [174] [Neutron stars with primary scalar hair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01937)
*Hamza Boumaza,Christos Charmousis,David Langlois,Etienne Ligout*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 研究了DHOST引力理论中具有原初标量毛的静态球对称中子星，通过求解修正TOV方程构建平衡态，发现正标量荷导致比GR更致密的星体，超过临界阈值会出现奇点，观测可约束这类理论参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索DHOST引力理论中中子星能否携带原初标量毛，以及标量毛对星体结构的影响，为检验修正引力理论提供天体物理探针。

Method: 在DHOST理论子类中求解修正TOV方程，采用多方态和真实物态方程构建平衡态构型，分析度规、标量场剖面及质量-半径关系。

Result: 正标量电荷导致比GR更致密的中子星；超过临界阈值时出现奇点；质量-半径关系偏离GR预言。

Conclusion: 观测可对DHOST理论中超越GR效应的参数及标量毛施加严格约束。

Abstract: We investigate static and spherically symmetric neutron star solutions endowed with primary scalar hair in a subfamily of Degenerate-Higher-Order-Scalar-Tensor (DHOST) theories of gravity. By solving the modified Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff (TOV) equations, we construct equilibrium configurations for polytropic and realistic equations of state and analyse the impact of the scalar hair on the stellar structure. We examine the resulting metric and scalar field profiles as well as the mass-radius relation, showing deviations from the predictions of General Relativity (GR). Positive scalar charges lead to more compact stars than in GR and, above a critical threshold, to singularities. Observations could therefore put stringent constraints on the parameters characterising the beyond-GR effects in these theories and their potential scalar hair.

</details>


### [175] [Boson Stars in Teleparallel Gravity with a Nonminimally Coupled Field: The Violation of Energy Conditions and Gravitational Waveforms from EMRIs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02017)
*Long-Xing Huang,Ke Yang,Yong-Qiang Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在非最小耦合的teleparallel引力框架下研究了玻色子星模型，获得了基态和激发态的静态球对称解。激发态解的能量密度可能为负，四种常见能量条件不再满足；而基态解能量密度为正，满足所有能量条件。此外，研究了由这些玻色子星组成的极端质量比旋进（EMRI）的引力波信号，发现其频域特征应变在LISA可探测范围内，可为区分致密天体提供潜在证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索teleparallel引力框架下非最小耦合玻色子星模型的理论性质，特别是基态与激发态解的差异，以及通过引力波信号为区分致密天体类型提供观测依据。

Method: 在非最小耦合teleparallel引力理论中构建玻色子星模型，数值求解获得基态和激发态的静态球对称解；分析能量密度和能量条件；进一步模拟极端质量比旋进（EMRI）系统，计算引力波频域特征应变并评估LISA的探测能力。

Result: 激发态解的能量密度可出现负值，四种常用能量条件不再满足；基态解能量密度始终为正，满足所有能量条件。EMRI系统的引力波频域特征应变位于LISA的可探测灵敏度范围内。

Conclusion: 非最小耦合teleparallel引力框架下的玻色子星模型存在基态和激发态解，两者在能量条件上存在本质差异；此类玻色子星产生的EMRI引力波信号有望被LISA探测，为区分致密天体提供潜在观测证据。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate boson star models within the framework of teleparallel gravity with non-minimal coupling, and obtain static, spherically symmetric solutions for both the ground state and excited states. The results indicate that the energy density of the excited-state solutions can become negative. For these solutions, the four commonly used energy conditions are no longer satisfied. In contrast, for all the ground-state solutions we have studied, the energy density remains positive and all four energy conditions are consistently satisfied. Moreover, considering the importance of astrophysical observations, the gravitational-wave signals from Extreme-Mass-Ratio Inspirals (EMRIs) composed of these boson stars are investigated. Our results reveal that the frequency-domain characteristic strain of these waveforms falls within the detectability range of LISA, which can provide potential evidence for distinguishing compact astrophysical objects.

</details>


### [176] [Nonlocal correlations of fermionic entanglement in the spacetime of Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02039)
*Yifei Xu,Yanjun Chen,Qi Xiao,Xiaolong Gong*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了d维球对称EGB黑洞时空中两种非经典相关性度量——量子相干非局域优势（NAQC）和贝尔非局域性（BN）随霍金温度的变化，发现两者均单调递减，且NAQC-BN层次关系在EGB时空中依然成立，揭示了高曲率修正对量子资源的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索弯曲时空中的非经典相关性，为量子信息理论与引力物理的交叉领域提供关键洞见。

Method: 考虑Alice和Rob初始共享最大纠缠Bell态，Alice自由落入黑洞（惯性Kruskal坐标系），Rob在视界外加速（非惯性Schwarzschild-like坐标系）。利用Unruh-Hawking效应修正Rob的场模式，通过Bogoliubov变换关联两个坐标系，推导费米子场的混合两体密度矩阵，并给出NAQC和BN的解析表达式。

Result: 两种相关性均随霍金温度（由参数α、d和r_h决定）升高而单调退化；NAQC-BN层次关系在EGB时空中依然保持。

Conclusion: 高曲率修正对量子资源产生显著影响，NAQC和BN在黑洞背景下表现出一致性退化行为。

Abstract: The investigation of nonclassical correlations in curved spacetimes offers key insights into the intersection of quantum information theory and gravitational physics. This paper studies two nonlocal correlation measures, non local advantage of quantum coherence (NAQC) and Bell nonlocality (BN) in a $d$-dimensional spherically symmetric Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet (EGB) black hole spacetime. We consider two observers (Alice and Rob) initially sharing a maximally entangled Bell state: Alice freely falls into the black hole (inertial Kruskal frame), while Rob accelerates outside the horizon (non-inertial Schwarzschild-like frame). The Unruh-Hawking effect modifies Rob's field modes, requiring Bogoliubov transformations to relate the two frames. We derive the mixed bipartite density matrix for fermionic fields and analytical expressions for NAQC and BN, which depend on Hawking temperature (itself governed by $α$, $d$, and $r_h$). Our results show both correlations degrade monotonically with increasing Hawking temperature, confirm the NAQC-BN hierarchical relationship persists in EGB spacetime, and highlighting the impact of high curvature corrections on quantum resources.

</details>


### [177] [The Direct Wave is Not a Meaningful Test of Horizon Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02380)
*Anuj Kankani,Sean T. McWilliams*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过数值相对论应变数据证明，黑洞并合辐射中的直接波（direct wave）频率与视界频率或表面引力无关，其阻尼时间显著演化，因此基于直接波检验霍金面积定律的方法不可靠，可能导致虚假的违反信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究将黑洞并合辐射中的直接波分量与剩余视界性质关联，并基于此对GW250114进行了霍金面积定律检验。本文旨在验证这种关联的可靠性。

Method: 使用数值相对论模拟的应变数据，分析直接波的瞬时频率和阻尼时间的演化特性；测试基于视界性质的演化频率模型在高自旋系统中的适用性。

Result: 直接波频率与视界频率/表面引力仅在χ_f≈0.7附近偶然交叉，无实质相关性；阻尼时间存在显著演化，单一阻尼正弦模型不适用；基于视界性质的演化频率模型对高自旋系统失效。

Conclusion: 直接波不能作为剩余视界性质的可靠探针，基于其进行的霍金面积定律检验可能导致虚假的违反结论。

Abstract: Recently, a distinct non--quasinormal mode component of black hole binary merger radiation, named the direct wave, has been identified. The frequency and damping time of the direct wave have been associated with properties of the remnant horizon. This has led to direct-wave based analysis of GW250114, including a test of Hawking's area law. However, as we demonstrate here using numerical relativity strain data, the direct wave frequency is not correlated with the horizon frequency or surface gravity, other than an incidental crossing around $χ_f \approx 0.7$ corresponding closely to the remnant spin of GW250114. We show that while the instantaneous frequency of the direct wave is quasi-stable, the damping time shows significant evolution and therefore a single damped sinusoid model, containing a fixed damping time, is not appropriate. We further show that an evolving frequency model based on horizon properties also does not model the direct wave component for systems with large remnant spins. We demonstrate that testing Hawking's area law with a horizon frequency based on the direct wave interpretation will lead to apparent violations of Hawking's area law when no violation actually occurs. Our results therefore indicate that the direct wave is not a reliable probe of the remnant horizon's properties.

</details>


### [178] [Black Hole Persistence in Scalar Tensor Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02409)
*Balkar Yildirim,Alan Albert Coley*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在标量-张量引力框架下，通过微扰方法构造了一个嵌入演化宇宙背景的中心不均匀性解，研究黑洞在非奇异反弹过程中的存续性。在辐射完美流体的反弹FLRW背景上，引入广义McVittie几何描述中心不均匀性，并在反弹点附近展开到O(η^4)求解场方程。解中存在三个任意函数，通过渐近FLRW条件约束。积分常数d_0作为真正的微扰参数，当d_0→0时所有微扰消失。最终发现一个随d_0尺度变化的小演化视界r_h~d_0，该视界在反弹中持续存在且演化不对称于η=0，支持黑洞在宇宙学转变中存续的解读。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究黑洞在非奇异宇宙反弹过程中的存续性，探讨标量-张量引力框架下中心不均匀性（潜在黑洞）能否穿越反弹阶段而不消失。

Method: 采用微扰方案（微扰参数ε），在辐射完美流体驱动的平坦FLRW反弹背景上，通过广义McVittie几何引入中心不均匀性。在反弹点η=0附近进行O(η^4)级数展开求解场方程，引入各向异性流体处理径向和切向压力，并通过渐近FLRW条件约束任意函数。

Result: 得到包含三个任意函数的完美流体解；积分常数d_0作为真正微扰参数；发现尺度为r_h~d_0的小演化视界，该视界在反弹中持续存在且演化关于η=0不对称。

Conclusion: 视界在反弹中的持续存在支持黑洞能够幸存于宇宙学转变（反弹）的物理解读，为反弹宇宙学中黑洞存续问题提供了微扰框架下的理论证据。

Abstract: We construct a perturbative scalar-tensor solution describing a central inhomogeneity embedded in an evolving cosmological background, with the aim of studying black hole persistence through a nonsingular bounce. Scalar-tensor gravity provides a natural framework for realizing bouncing cosmologies, while the inclusion of a localized inhomogeneity makes the field equations substantially more difficult to solve. We therefore adopt a perturbative scheme, with perturbative parameter $ε$, in which the leading-order equations are solved by a spatially flat bouncing FLRW spacetime sourced by a radiation perfect fluid. At next order, a central inhomogeneity is introduced through a generalized McVittie geometry, with the perturbations encoded in the corresponding first-order metric and scalar-field functions. We first allow an anisotropic fluid with radial and tangential pressures, whose diagonal components solve the diagonal field equations. The field equations are solved as a series expansion up to $\mathcal{O}(η^4)$ near the bounce at $η=0$. The resulting perfect fluid solution contains three arbitrary functions which are constrained by requiring the spacetime to asymptote to FLRW as $r\to\infty$. With suitable initial conditions preserving the parabolic structure of the bounce, the integration constant $d_0$ emerges as the true perturbative parameter: all perturbations vanish as $d_0\to0$. Finally, we find a small evolving horizon, $r_h\sim d_0$, which we interpret as the horizon of the central inhomogeneity. Its persistence through the bounce supports the interpretation of a black hole surviving the cosmological transition, and its evolution is not symmetric about $η=0$.

</details>
